# LLMAnalyst Project History

Generated: 2026-08-17 09:53:59
Location: /home/ajay/LLMAnalyst

## Project Purpose
Self-contained web application that scrapes Ollama's library, groups models into plain-English categories, tags cloud availability and Hermes/Claw compatibility, and presents them in a beautiful card view plus a full filterable/sortable table.

## Key Design Decisions
- Cloud availability sourced from Ollama's own `cloud` badge (not OpenRouter).
- Hermes compatibility = works with Hermes/Claw/agent stack; detected via Ollama `tools` tag + known Hermes-family list + description mentions.
- Purpose categories: Coding, Research & Deep Thinking, General Chat / Assistant, Vision / Multimodal, Embeddings, Math & STEM, Creative / Roleplay.
- Default sort: pull_value descending, with recency tie-break via estimated_updated_iso.
- Dark mode persists in localStorage and falls back to prefers-color-scheme.
- Model detail shown in modal, not separate page, to keep it simple.
- Daily rescrape scheduled via cron at 03:00.
- Cannot be pure GitHub Pages because FastAPI backend is required for /api/models, /api/refresh, /api/health.

## Feature Evolution
1. Initial FastAPI server + scraper + basic card/table UI.
2. Added cache health, custom 404 handler, background rescrape.
3. Added favourites, recency chips, CSV/JSON export, modal size selector, keyboard shortcuts, auto-refresh on focus, category colour coding, "Not Hermes/Claw" filter, copy-toast, loading skeletons.
4. Added hero chart, copy-link-to-filters, per-category icons, Open Graph/Twitter tags, skip link, modal focus trap, model permalinks, Fuse.js fuzzy fallback, model compare, PWA manifest + service worker, daily cron rescrape, new-model notification banner.
5. Visual polish: Poppins font, Ollama-aligned indigo/purple palette, gradients, frosted-glass header, card/modal animations.
6. UX fixes: grouped filters, consistent chips, active filters bar, URL query state, debounced search.
7. Accessibility: aria-labels, scope=col, scope=row, focus-visible, skip link.
8. Table fixes: forced nowrap, icon-only compact columns, full-width table page with horizontal scroll.

## Known Issues Resolved
- Raw HTML entities (&lt;) in JS templates replaced with real characters.
- Pull counts empty on some models: scraper now captures pull_value from library listing page.
- Missing metadata for gemma4 / granite4.1-guardian: normalized joined date strings and accepted "Downloads" as pull synonym.
- Server cache age parsing: switched to calendar.timegm().
- Updated column sorted alphabetically on "2 years ago" strings: changed to sort by ISO timestamp estimated_updated_iso.
- Capability empty cell misalignment: added .caps-wrap / .caps-empty with explicit sizing.
- Table wider than page: widened container and enabled horizontal scroll on table page.

## Deployment Constraints
- Requires Python backend (FastAPI + scraper).
- Options: (1) run on VPS/home server/Fly.io/Railway/Render; (2) static export via CI; (3) hybrid static frontend + JSON hosted elsewhere.

## Commands to Run
```bash
cd /home/ajay/LLMAnalyst
. .venv/bin/activate
python server.py
```

Tests:
```bash
python tests/validation.py
```

## Pre-Launch Checklist
- [ ] Replace https://llmanalyst.example.com in static/index.html and static/table.html with real domain.
- [ ] Create/upload real static/og-image.png (1200x630 recommended).
- [ ] Verify deployment target and update DEPENDENCIES.md if needed.
- [ ] Run validation tests: python tests/validation.py
